> Original PDF at source-pdfs/assessor-manual/stc-assessors-manual-vol2-18unit-in-place-costs-1-5.pdf, text via pdftotext -layout.

> figures, but ⚠ ALWAYS VERIFY A COST FIGURE AGAINST THE PDF before using it in a
> valuation — a mis-parsed table cell is indistinguishable from a correct one in text.
> Volume I = residential and agricultural. Volume II = commercial and industrial.

in the roof structure costs. In plain buildings such as industrials and                  surfaces by the costs given below.

ances where special ceiling framing is needed.                                           sification. As a guide, small or neighborhood cinemas will usually fit

as noted.
